# Agents · P · part 27

912 entries, product-manager to product-strategy. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **product-manager**: Orchestrates development of stories queued in `.user-stories/` by product-owner, one at a time, through analyst, dev-frontend, dev-backend, dev-integrator, story-validator, and doc-writer. Tracks progress and pauses safely when needed. Errors out if `.user-stories/` is empty. Run this independently, as the session agent (claude --agent product-manager), not as a spawned subagent. Supports ONE STORY MODE and WORKTREE MODE for the helper scripts in scripts/, and an optional STORY LIMIT for manual invocations — defaults to the whole queue when not given. ([marobert-dev/claude-code](https://github.com/marobert-dev/claude-code/blob/HEAD/agents/product-manager.md))
